**Yeni dosya:** `app/ui/search/model.ts`

````ts
// Harita aramasının arayüz modeli (ileri modüller spec'i §12.1): koşul türleri, yeni koşulun
// başlangıç değeri, biçim denetimi ve eşleşen olgunun kısa özeti. Burada hesap yoktur (C4):
// koşulları Rust değerlendirir, olgular Rust'tan gelir; buradaki her şey seçim listesi, biçim
// denetimi ve gösterimdir. Sorgu ve sonuçlar loglanmaz.
import type { TFunction } from "i18next";
import type { ChartQuery, Condition, Dignity, GrahaFlag, KarakaRole, MatchFact, Paksha, QueryCondition, QueryScope, ScopeKind, Sign, SkyEventKind } from "../bindings";
import { signIndex, SIGNS, VARGAS } from "../chart/adapter";
import { GRAHA_IDS, type GrahaId } from "../chart/types";
import { arudhaLabel, KARAKA_ROLES, nakshatraName, tithiName, tithiShort } from "../panels/labels";

/** Rust `Condition`'ın on üç türü, formdaki sırayla. */
export const CONDITION_KINDS = [
  "GrahaInSign",
  "GrahaInHouse",
  "GrahaInNakshatra",
  "GrahaState",
  "LagnaInSign",
  "Conjunction",
  "GrahaAspects",
  "HouseLordInHouse",
  "MoonPhase",
  "DashaAt",
  "CharaKaraka",
  "ArudhaInSign",
  "YogaPresent",
] as const;
export type ConditionKind = (typeof CONDITION_KINDS)[number];

/** Rust `GrahaFlag::ALL`. */
export const GRAHA_FLAGS = ["Retrograde", "Combust", "Vargottama"] as const satisfies readonly GrahaFlag[];
export const DIGNITIES = ["Exalted", "Moolatrikona", "OwnSign", "GreatFriend", "Friend", "Neutral", "Enemy", "GreatEnemy", "Debilitated"] as const satisfies readonly Dignity[];
/** Rust `EIGHT_KARAKA_ROLES`. */
export const KARAKA_ROLE_IDS = ["Atma", "Amatya", "Bhratri", "Matri", "Pitri", "Putra", "Gnati", "Dara"] as const satisfies readonly KarakaRole[];
export const PAKSHAS = ["Shukla", "Krishna"] as const satisfies readonly Paksha[];
export const SCOPE_KINDS = ["Natal", "Event"] as const satisfies readonly ScopeKind[];
/** Tam bakış atan grahalar (Rust `ASPECTING_GRAHAS`): düğümler bakmaz. */
export const ASPECTING = GRAHA_IDS.slice(0, 7);
/** Rust `SkyEventKind::ALL`. */
export const EVENT_KINDS = [
  "SignIngress",
  "NakshatraIngress",
  "StationRetrograde",
  "StationDirect",
  "Conjunction",
  "NewMoon",
  "FullMoon",
  "TransitToNatal",
  "HouseFromNatalMoon",
] as const satisfies readonly SkyEventKind[];
export const HOUSES = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => i + 1);
export const NAKSHATRAS = Array.from({ length: 27 }, (_, i) => i + 1);
export const TITHIS = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => i + 1);
export const PADAS = [1, 2, 3, 4];
/** Rust `MAX_CONDITIONS`, `MAX_SCOPE_TAGS`, `MAX_YOGA_IDS`, `MAX_SEARCH_DASHA_LEVEL`, `MAX_SKY_EVENTS`. */
export const MAX_CONDITIONS = 12;
export const MAX_SCOPE_TAGS = 16;
export const MAX_YOGA_IDS = 16;
export const MAX_DASHA_LEVEL = 3;
export const MAX_EVENTS = 2000;
/** Rust `store::MAX_SAVED_SEARCHES`. */
export const MAX_SAVED_SEARCHES = 64;

export const EMPTY_SCOPE: QueryScope = { tags: [], kind: null, exclude_prashna: true };
export const EMPTY_QUERY: ChartQuery = { all_of: [], scope: EMPTY_SCOPE };

export function kindOf(cond: Condition): ConditionKind {
  return CONDITION_KINDS.find((k) => k in cond) ?? "GrahaInSign";
}

/** Yeni eklenen koşulun başlangıç değeri: kümeler boştur ve astrolog doldurur. */
export function defaultCondition(kind: ConditionKind): Condition {
  switch (kind) {
    case "GrahaInSign":
      return { GrahaInSign: { graha: "Sun", signs: [], varga: "D1" } };
    case "GrahaInHouse":
      return { GrahaInHouse: { graha: "Sun", houses: [] } };
    case "GrahaInNakshatra":
      return { GrahaInNakshatra: { graha: "Moon", nakshatras: [], padas: [] } };
    case "GrahaState":
      return { GrahaState: { graha: "Mars", flags: [], dignities: [] } };
    case "LagnaInSign":
      return { LagnaInSign: { signs: [], varga: "D1" } };
    case "Conjunction":
      return { Conjunction: { grahas: [] } };
    case "GrahaAspects":
      return { GrahaAspects: { from: "Saturn", to: "Moon" } };
    case "HouseLordInHouse":
      return { HouseLordInHouse: { lord_of: 1, houses: [] } };
    case "MoonPhase":
      return { MoonPhase: { tithis: [], paksha: null } };
    case "DashaAt":
      return { DashaAt: { date: null, level: 1, lords: [] } };
    case "CharaKaraka":
      return { CharaKaraka: { role: "Atma", grahas: [] } };
    case "ArudhaInSign":
      return { ArudhaInSign: { pada: 1, signs: [] } };
    case "YogaPresent":
      return { YogaPresent: { ids: [] } };
  }
}

/** "Örnek koşul": boş formun tek eyleminin eklediği, hemen aranabilen koşul. */
export const EXAMPLE_CONDITION: QueryCondition = { cond: { GrahaInSign: { graha: "Moon", signs: ["Cancer"], varga: "D1" } }, negate: false };

const DATE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

/**
 * Koşulun eksiği (i18n anahtarı `search.issue.<değer>`); tamamsa `null`. Yalnızca biçim
 * denetimidir: aramayı başlatmadan önce hangi satırın eksik olduğunu söyler. Asıl denetim
 * Rust'tadır (`ChartQuery::validate`).
 */
export function conditionIssue(cond: Condition): string | null {
  if (cond.GrahaInSign) return cond.GrahaInSign.signs.length === 0 ? "signs" : null;
  if (cond.GrahaInHouse) return cond.GrahaInHouse.houses.length === 0 ? "houses" : null;
  if (cond.GrahaInNakshatra) return cond.GrahaInNakshatra.nakshatras.length === 0 ? "nakshatras" : null;
  if (cond.GrahaState) return cond.GrahaState.flags.length + cond.GrahaState.dignities.length === 0 ? "states" : null;
  if (cond.LagnaInSign) return cond.LagnaInSign.signs.length === 0 ? "signs" : null;
  if (cond.Conjunction) return cond.Conjunction.grahas.length < 2 ? "twoGrahas" : null;
  if (cond.GrahaAspects) return cond.GrahaAspects.from === cond.GrahaAspects.to ? "sameGraha" : null;
  if (cond.HouseLordInHouse) return cond.HouseLordInHouse.houses.length === 0 ? "houses" : null;
  if (cond.MoonPhase) return cond.MoonPhase.tithis.length === 0 && cond.MoonPhase.paksha === null ? "phase" : null;
  if (cond.DashaAt) return cond.DashaAt.lords.length === 0 ? "lords" : cond.DashaAt.date !== null && !DATE.test(cond.DashaAt.date) ? "date" : null;
  if (cond.CharaKaraka) return cond.CharaKaraka.grahas.length === 0 ? "grahas" : null;
  if (cond.ArudhaInSign) return cond.ArudhaInSign.signs.length === 0 ? "signs" : null;
  if (cond.YogaPresent) return cond.YogaPresent.ids.length === 0 ? "yogas" : cond.YogaPresent.ids.length > MAX_YOGA_IDS ? "tooManyYogas" : null;
  return null;
}

/** Sorgunun aranmasını engelleyen ilk eksik: (satır sırası, anahtar); yoksa `null`. */
export function queryIssue(query: ChartQuery): { row: number; issue: string } | null {
  for (const [row, c] of query.all_of.entries()) {
    const issue = conditionIssue(c.cond);
    if (issue !== null) return { row, issue };
  }
  return null;
}

/** Sorgunun kimliği: sonuç listesi başlatıldığı sorguya aittir. */
export function queryKey(query: ChartQuery): string {
  return JSON.stringify(query);
}

/** Kümeye ekler ya da çıkarır; sonuç `order` sırasındadır. */
export function toggle<T>(set: readonly T[], value: T, order: readonly T[]): T[] {
  const next = set.includes(value) ? set.filter((v) => v !== value) : [...set, value];
  return order.filter((v) => next.includes(v));
}

/** Virgülle ayrılmış etiket metni → etiket listesi (kırpılmış, boşlar atılmış, en çok `MAX_SCOPE_TAGS`). */
export function parseTags(text: string): string[] {
  return text
    .split(",")
    .map((t) => t.trim())
    .filter((t) => t !== "")
    .slice(0, MAX_SCOPE_TAGS);
}

/** Yoga kimlikleri, i18n kaynağındaki `yogas.name` ağacından (`grup.ad`), katalog sırasıyla. */
export function yogaIds(names: Record<string, Record<string, string>>): string[] {
  return Object.entries(names).flatMap(([group, rules]) => Object.keys(rules).map((rule) => `${group}.${rule}`));
}

const sign = (t: TFunction, s: Sign) => t(`sign.${signIndex(s)}`);
const graha = (t: TFunction, g: GrahaId) => t(`graha.${g}`);

/** Koşulun kısa yazımı ("Gü 1/10. ev"): "değil" olgusunun ve satır özetinin metni. */
export function conditionBrief(t: TFunction, cond: Condition): string {
  const signs = (list: readonly Sign[]) => list.map((s) => sign(t, s)).join("/");
  const grahas = (list: readonly GrahaId[]) => list.map((g) => graha(t, g)).join("/");
  const varga = (v: string) => (v === "D1" ? "" : ` ${v}`);
  if (cond.GrahaInSign) return `${graha(t, cond.GrahaInSign.graha)}${varga(cond.GrahaInSign.varga)} ${signs(cond.GrahaInSign.signs)}`;
  if (cond.GrahaInHouse) return t("search.brief.house", { what: graha(t, cond.GrahaInHouse.graha), houses: cond.GrahaInHouse.houses.join("/") });
  if (cond.GrahaInNakshatra) return `${graha(t, cond.GrahaInNakshatra.graha)} ${cond.GrahaInNakshatra.nakshatras.map(nakshatraName).join("/")}`;
  if (cond.GrahaState) {
    const states = [...cond.GrahaState.flags.map((f) => t(`search.flag.${f}`)), ...cond.GrahaState.dignities.map((d) => t(`dignity.${d}`))];
    return `${graha(t, cond.GrahaState.graha)} ${states.join("/")}`;
  }
  if (cond.LagnaInSign) return `${t("chart.lagna")}${varga(cond.LagnaInSign.varga)} ${signs(cond.LagnaInSign.signs)}`;
  if (cond.Conjunction) return cond.Conjunction.grahas.map((g) => graha(t, g)).join("+");
  if (cond.GrahaAspects) return `${graha(t, cond.GrahaAspects.from)} → ${graha(t, cond.GrahaAspects.to)}`;
  if (cond.HouseLordInHouse) return t("search.brief.house", { what: t("search.brief.lordOf", { house: cond.HouseLordInHouse.lord_of }), houses: cond.HouseLordInHouse.houses.join("/") });
  if (cond.MoonPhase) return [...cond.MoonPhase.tithis.map(tithiShort), ...(cond.MoonPhase.paksha ? [cond.MoonPhase.paksha] : [])].join("/");
  if (cond.DashaAt) return t("search.brief.dasha", { level: cond.DashaAt.level, lords: grahas(cond.DashaAt.lords) });
  if (cond.CharaKaraka) return `${KARAKA_ROLES[cond.CharaKaraka.role].short} ${grahas(cond.CharaKaraka.grahas)}`;
  if (cond.ArudhaInSign) return `${arudhaLabel(cond.ArudhaInSign.pada)} ${signs(cond.ArudhaInSign.signs)}`;
  if (cond.YogaPresent) return cond.YogaPresent.ids.map((id) => t(`yogas.name.${id}`)).join("/");
  return "";
}

/**
 * Eşleşen olgunun kısa özeti ("Ma Oğ 10. ev", "Jü R", "değil: Gü 1/10. ev"). Retro düz `R`'dir;
 * Rust düğümlerde retro işaretini hiç göndermez.
 */
export function factSummary(t: TFunction, cond: Condition, fact: MatchFact): string {
  if (fact.negated) return t("search.fact.not", { what: conditionBrief(t, cond) });
  const g = fact.grahas.map((x) => graha(t, x));
  const s = fact.sign === null ? "" : sign(t, fact.sign);
  const house = fact.house === null ? "" : ` ${t("search.fact.house", { house: fact.house })}`;
  const retro = fact.retrograde ? " R" : "";
  if (cond.GrahaInSign && cond.GrahaInSign.varga !== "D1") return `${g[0]} ${cond.GrahaInSign.varga} ${s}`;
  if (cond.GrahaInSign || cond.GrahaInHouse || cond.HouseLordInHouse) return `${g[0]} ${s}${house}${retro}`;
  if (cond.GrahaInNakshatra) return `${g[0]} ${fact.nakshatra === null ? "" : nakshatraName(fact.nakshatra)} ${fact.pada ?? ""}`.trimEnd();
  if (cond.GrahaState) {
    // Retro durumu `R` ile yazılır; yanık ve vargottama sözle, dignity kendi adıyla.
    const states = [...fact.flags.filter((f) => f !== "Retrograde").map((f) => t(`search.flag.${f}`)), ...(fact.dignity ? [t(`dignity.${fact.dignity}`)] : [])];
    return `${g[0]} ${s}${retro}${states.length > 0 ? ` ${states.join(", ")}` : ""}`;
  }
  if (cond.LagnaInSign) return `${t("chart.lagna")}${cond.LagnaInSign.varga === "D1" ? "" : ` ${cond.LagnaInSign.varga}`} ${s}`;
  if (cond.Conjunction) return `${g.join("+")} ${s}${house}`;
  if (cond.GrahaAspects) return `${g[0]} → ${g[1]}`;
  if (cond.MoonPhase) return fact.tithi === null ? "" : `${tithiShort(fact.tithi)} ${tithiName(fact.tithi)}`;
  if (cond.DashaAt) return g.join("–");
  if (cond.CharaKaraka) return `${KARAKA_ROLES[cond.CharaKaraka.role].short} ${g[0]}`;
  if (cond.ArudhaInSign) return `${arudhaLabel(cond.ArudhaInSign.pada)} ${s}`;
  if (cond.YogaPresent) return fact.yogas.map((id) => t(`yogas.name.${id}`)).join(", ");
  return "";
}

/** Bir sonucun özet satırı: koşul sırasıyla olgular, "; " ile. Sorguda karşılığı olmayan olgu atlanır. */
export function hitSummary(t: TFunction, query: ChartQuery, matched: readonly MatchFact[]): string {
  return matched
    .map((f) => {
      const c = query.all_of[f.condition];
      return c ? factSummary(t, c.cond, f) : "";
    })
    .filter((s) => s !== "")
    .join("; ");
}

export { GRAHA_IDS, SIGNS, VARGAS };
````

**Yeni dosya:** `app/ui/search/ids.ts`

````ts
// Arama kimlikleri (ileri modüller spec'i §12): her harita ve olay araması yeni bir kimlik
// alır. Rust, durdurulan ya da yerine yenisi başlatılan aramanın isteğini bu kimlikle reddeder.
let last = 0;

/** Bu oturumda daha önce verilmemiş bir kimlik (u32'ye sığar). */
export function nextSearchId(): number {
  last = last >= 0xffff_fffe ? 1 : last + 1;
  return last;
}
````

**Yeni dosya:** `app/ui/search/useChartSearch.ts`

````ts
// Harita aramasını (`chart_search`) yürüten kanca (ileri modüller spec'i §12.1). Arama sayfa
// sayfa ilerler: her yanıtın imleci bir sonraki isteğe verilir; eşleşmeler geldikçe listeye
// eklenir. Her arama yeni bir kimlik alır: eski aramanın geç gelen yanıtı ve sonraki isteği
// düşer (kimlik hem burada hem Rust'ta denetlenir). "Durdur" Rust'a bildirilir; gelmiş
// eşleşmeler ve sayılar yerinde durur. Arayüzde hesap yoktur (C4); sonuç saklanmaz (K1).
// Sorgu ve sonuçlar (adlar, sayılar, kimlikler) loglanmaz.
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "../api";
import type { ChartHit, ChartQuery, ChartSearchCursor, CommandError } from "../bindings";
import { nextSearchId } from "./ids";

export type ChartSearchStatus = "idle" | "running" | "done" | "stopped" | "error";

export interface ChartSearchState {
  status: ChartSearchStatus;
  /** Listenin ait olduğu sorgu (başlatıldığı andaki kopya); arama yokken `null`. */
  query: ChartQuery | null;
  hits: ChartHit[];
  /** Taranan ve kapsamdaki harita sayısı; ilk yanıttan önce `total` bilinmez (`null`). */
  scanned: number;
  total: number | null;
  skippedTimeUnknown: number;
  failed: number;
  error: CommandError | null;
}

export interface ChartSearch extends ChartSearchState {
  start(query: ChartQuery): void;
  /** Aramayı durdurur; gelmiş eşleşmeler durur. */
  stop(): void;
  /** Sonuçları siler (form temizlenince). */
  reset(): void;
}

const IDLE: ChartSearchState = { status: "idle", query: null, hits: [], scanned: 0, total: null, skippedTimeUnknown: 0, failed: 0, error: null };

export function useChartSearch(): ChartSearch {
  const [state, setState] = useState<ChartSearchState>(IDLE);
  // Süren aramanın kimliği; `null`: süren arama yok. Yanıt yalnızca kimliği buradakiyle aynıysa
  // işlenir.
  const live = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const cancel = useCallback(() => {
    const id = live.current;
    live.current = null;
    // Rust süren sayfayı dilim sınırında bırakır; yanıtı beklenmez.
    if (id !== null) void api.chartSearchCancel(id);
  }, []);

  // Yüzey kapanırken süren arama durdurulur.
  useEffect(() => cancel, [cancel]);

  const start = useCallback(
    (query: ChartQuery) => {
      cancel();
      const id = nextSearchId();
      live.current = id;
      setState({ ...IDLE, status: "running", query });
      void (async () => {
        let cursor: ChartSearchCursor | null = null;
        for (;;) {
          const r = await api.chartSearch(query, id, cursor);
          if (live.current !== id) return;
          if (r.status === "error") {
            live.current = null;
            // Gelmiş eşleşmeler durur; hata ayrıca gösterilir.
            setState((s) => ({ ...s, status: "error", error: r.error }));
            return;
          }
          const page = r.data;
          cursor = page.next;
          if (page.done) live.current = null;
          setState((s) => ({
            status: page.done ? "done" : "running",
            query,
            hits: [...s.hits, ...page.hits],
            scanned: page.scanned,
            total: page.total,
            skippedTimeUnknown: page.skipped_time_unknown,
            failed: page.failed,
            error: null,
          }));
          if (page.done || cursor === null) return;
        }
      })();
    },
    [cancel],
  );

  const stop = useCallback(() => {
    if (live.current === null) return;
    cancel();
    setState((s) => (s.status === "running" ? { ...s, status: "stopped" } : s));
  }, [cancel]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    cancel();
    setState(IDLE);
  }, [cancel]);

  return { ...state, start, stop, reset };
}
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Yüzey: seçenek kümesi, koşul satırı, pencere ve stil**

Kullanılamayan düğme yerel `disabled` değildir: odaklanır ve nedeni bağlı bir satırda yazılıdır. Kalkan her düğme (silinen satırın "Koşulu sil"i, "Durdur", "Yeniden dene", "Aramayı sil") odağını pencerede duran bir öğeye bırakır.

**Yeni dosya:** `app/ui/search/CheckSet.tsx`

````tsx
// Onay kutusu kümesi: görünür başlık ve her değer için etiketli bir kutu. Uzun kümeler açılır
// kapanır bir bölümde durur; bölümün başlığı seçili sayıyı söyler.
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

export interface CheckSetProps<T extends string | number> {
  legend: string;
  options: readonly { value: T; label: ReactNode }[];
  selected: readonly T[];
  onToggle(value: T): void;
  /** Verilirse küme `<details>` içinde durur ve bu metin özet satırıdır. */
  fold?: string;
}

export function CheckSet<T extends string | number>({ legend, options, selected, onToggle, fold }: CheckSetProps<T>) {
  const boxes = (
    <fieldset className="search-checks">
      <legend className={fold ? "visually-hidden" : undefined}>{legend}</legend>
      {options.map((o) => (
        <label key={o.value}>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={selected.includes(o.value)} onChange={() => onToggle(o.value)} />
          {o.label}
        </label>
      ))}
    </fieldset>
  );
  if (!fold) return boxes;
  return (
    <details className="search-fold">
      <summary>{fold}</summary>
      {boxes}
    </details>
  );
}
````

**Yeni dosya:** `app/ui/search/ConditionRow.tsx`

````tsx
// Harita aramasının bir koşul satırı (ileri modüller spec'i §12.1): tür seçici, türe göre
// parametre alanları (görünür etiketli), "değil" kutusu ve satırı sil. Alanlar yalnızca seçimi
// toplar; koşulu Rust değerlendirir (C4). Retro, Rahu ve Ketu için sunulmaz.
import { useId, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import type { Condition, Graha, QueryCondition, Sign, Varga } from "../bindings";
import { signIndex } from "../chart/adapter";
import { tr } from "../i18n/tr";
import { arudhaLabel, KARAKA_ROLES, nakshatraName, tithiName, tithiShort } from "../panels/labels";
import { CheckSet } from "./CheckSet";
import {
  ASPECTING,
  CONDITION_KINDS,
  conditionIssue,
  defaultCondition,
  DIGNITIES,
  GRAHA_FLAGS,
  GRAHA_IDS,
  HOUSES,
  KARAKA_ROLE_IDS,
  kindOf,
  MAX_DASHA_LEVEL,
  NAKSHATRAS,
  PADAS,
  PAKSHAS,
  SIGNS,
  TITHIS,
  toggle,
  VARGAS,
  yogaIds,
  type ConditionKind,
} from "./model";

const YOGA_IDS = yogaIds(tr.yogas.name);
const LEVELS = Array.from({ length: MAX_DASHA_LEVEL }, (_, i) => i + 1);

export interface ConditionRowProps {
  index: number;
  value: QueryCondition;
  onChange(value: QueryCondition): void;
  onRemove(): void;
}

export function ConditionRow({ index, value, onChange, onRemove }: ConditionRowProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const id = useId();
  const { cond } = value;
  const kind = kindOf(cond);
  const issue = conditionIssue(cond);
  const set = (next: Condition) => onChange({ ...value, cond: next });
  const count = (n: number) => t(n === 0 ? "search.noneSelected" : "search.selected", { n });

  const select = <T extends string | number>(key: string, label: string, current: T, options: readonly { value: T; label: string }[], pick: (v: T) => void): ReactNode => (
    <span className="search-field">
      <label htmlFor={`${id}-${key}`}>{label}</label>
      <select
        id={`${id}-${key}`}
        value={current}
        onChange={(e) => {
          const found = options.find((o) => String(o.value) === e.target.value);
          if (found) pick(found.value);
        }}
      >
        {options.map((o) => (
          <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>
            {o.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </span>
  );
  const grahaOptions = (list: readonly Graha[]) => list.map((g) => ({ value: g, label: t(`grahaName.${g}`) }));
  const grahaSelect = (key: string, label: string, current: Graha, pick: (g: Graha) => void, list: readonly Graha[] = GRAHA_IDS) => select(key, label, current, grahaOptions(list), pick);
  const vargaSelect = (current: Varga, pick: (v: Varga) => void) =>
    select(
      "varga",
      t("search.field.varga"),
      current,
      VARGAS.map((v) => ({ value: v, label: v })),
      pick,
    );
  const houseSelect = (key: string, label: string, current: number, pick: (h: number) => void, name: (h: number) => string = String) =>
    select(
      key,
      label,
      current,
      HOUSES.map((h) => ({ value: h, label: name(h) })),
      pick,
    );
  const signSet = (selected: readonly Sign[], pick: (signs: Sign[]) => void) => (
    <CheckSet
      legend={t("search.field.signs")}
      fold={`${t("search.field.signs")} · ${count(selected.length)}`}
      options={SIGNS.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: t(`sign.${signIndex(s)}`) }))}
      selected={selected}
      onToggle={(s) => pick(toggle(selected, s, SIGNS))}
    />
  );
  const houseSet = (selected: readonly number[], pick: (houses: number[]) => void) => (
    <CheckSet
      legend={t("search.field.houses")}
      fold={`${t("search.field.houses")} · ${count(selected.length)}`}
      options={HOUSES.map((h) => ({ value: h, label: String(h) }))}
      selected={selected}
      onToggle={(h) => pick(toggle(selected, h, HOUSES))}
    />
  );
  const grahaSet = (legend: string, selected: readonly Graha[], pick: (grahas: Graha[]) => void) => (
    <CheckSet
      legend={legend}
      fold={`${legend} · ${count(selected.length)}`}
      options={grahaOptions(GRAHA_IDS)}
      selected={selected}
      onToggle={(g) => pick(toggle(selected, g, GRAHA_IDS))}
    />
  );

  let fields: ReactNode = null;
  if (cond.GrahaInSign) {
    const c = cond.GrahaInSign;
    fields = (
      <>
        {grahaSelect("graha", t("search.field.graha"), c.graha, (graha) => set({ GrahaInSign: { ...c, graha } }))}
        {vargaSelect(c.varga, (varga) => set({ GrahaInSign: { ...c, varga } }))}
        {signSet(c.signs, (signs) => set({ GrahaInSign: { ...c, signs } }))}
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.GrahaInHouse) {
    const c = cond.GrahaInHouse;
    fields = (
      <>
        {grahaSelect("graha", t("search.field.graha"), c.graha, (graha) => set({ GrahaInHouse: { ...c, graha } }))}
        {houseSet(c.houses, (houses) => set({ GrahaInHouse: { ...c, houses } }))}
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.GrahaInNakshatra) {
    const c = cond.GrahaInNakshatra;
    fields = (
      <>
        {grahaSelect("graha", t("search.field.graha"), c.graha, (graha) => set({ GrahaInNakshatra: { ...c, graha } }))}
        <CheckSet
          legend={t("search.field.nakshatras")}
          fold={`${t("search.field.nakshatras")} · ${count(c.nakshatras.length)}`}
          options={NAKSHATRAS.map((n) => ({ value: n, label: nakshatraName(n) }))}
          selected={c.nakshatras}
          onToggle={(n) => set({ GrahaInNakshatra: { ...c, nakshatras: toggle(c.nakshatras, n, NAKSHATRAS) } })}
        />
        <CheckSet
          legend={t("search.field.padas")}
          options={PADAS.map((p) => ({ value: p, label: String(p) }))}
          selected={c.padas}
          onToggle={(p) => set({ GrahaInNakshatra: { ...c, padas: toggle(c.padas, p, PADAS) } })}
        />
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.GrahaState) {
    const c = cond.GrahaState;
    // Düğümlerde retro sunulmaz; graha düğüme çevrilince işaretli retro kalkar.
    const flags = GRAHA_FLAGS.filter((f) => f !== "Retrograde" || (c.graha !== "Rahu" && c.graha !== "Ketu"));
    fields = (
      <>
        {grahaSelect("graha", t("search.field.graha"), c.graha, (graha) =>
          set({ GrahaState: { ...c, graha, flags: graha === "Rahu" || graha === "Ketu" ? c.flags.filter((f) => f !== "Retrograde") : c.flags } }),
        )}
        <CheckSet
          legend={t("search.field.flags")}
          options={flags.map((f) => ({ value: f, label: f === "Retrograde" ? "R" : t(`search.flag.${f}`) }))}
          selected={c.flags}
          onToggle={(f) => set({ GrahaState: { ...c, flags: toggle(c.flags, f, GRAHA_FLAGS) } })}
        />
        <CheckSet
          legend={t("search.field.dignities")}
          fold={`${t("search.field.dignities")} · ${count(c.dignities.length)}`}
          options={DIGNITIES.map((d) => ({ value: d, label: t(`dignity.${d}`) }))}
          selected={c.dignities}
          onToggle={(d) => set({ GrahaState: { ...c, dignities: toggle(c.dignities, d, DIGNITIES) } })}
        />
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.LagnaInSign) {
    const c = cond.LagnaInSign;
    fields = (
      <>
        {vargaSelect(c.varga, (varga) => set({ LagnaInSign: { ...c, varga } }))}
        {signSet(c.signs, (signs) => set({ LagnaInSign: { ...c, signs } }))}
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.Conjunction) {
    const c = cond.Conjunction;
    fields = grahaSet(t("search.field.grahas"), c.grahas, (grahas) => set({ Conjunction: { grahas } }));
  } else if (cond.GrahaAspects) {
    const c = cond.GrahaAspects;
    fields = (
      <>
        {grahaSelect("from", t("search.field.from"), c.from, (from) => set({ GrahaAspects: { ...c, from } }), ASPECTING)}
        {grahaSelect("to", t("search.field.to"), c.to, (to) => set({ GrahaAspects: { ...c, to } }))}
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.HouseLordInHouse) {
    const c = cond.HouseLordInHouse;
    fields = (
      <>
        {houseSelect("lord", t("search.field.lordOf"), c.lord_of, (lord_of) => set({ HouseLordInHouse: { ...c, lord_of } }))}
        {houseSet(c.houses, (houses) => set({ HouseLordInHouse: { ...c, houses } }))}
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.MoonPhase) {
    const c = cond.MoonPhase;
    fields = (
      <>
        {select(
          "paksha",
          t("search.field.paksha"),
          c.paksha ?? "",
          [{ value: "" as const, label: t("search.field.pakshaAny") }, ...PAKSHAS.map((p) => ({ value: p, label: p }))],
          (paksha) => set({ MoonPhase: { ...c, paksha: paksha === "" ? null : paksha } }),
        )}
        <CheckSet
          legend={t("search.field.tithis")}
          fold={`${t("search.field.tithis")} · ${count(c.tithis.length)}`}
          options={TITHIS.map((n) => ({ value: n, label: `${tithiShort(n)} ${tithiName(n)}` }))}
          selected={c.tithis}
          onToggle={(n) => set({ MoonPhase: { ...c, tithis: toggle(c.tithis, n, TITHIS) } })}
        />
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.DashaAt) {
    const c = cond.DashaAt;
    fields = (
      <>
        <span className="search-field">
          <label htmlFor={`${id}-date`}>{t("search.field.date")}</label>
          <input id={`${id}-date`} type="date" value={c.date ?? ""} aria-describedby={`${id}-date-hint`} onChange={(e) => set({ DashaAt: { ...c, date: e.target.value === "" ? null : e.target.value } })} />
          <span id={`${id}-date-hint`} className="chart-toolbar-hint">
            {t("search.field.dateHint")}
          </span>
        </span>
        {select(
          "level",
          t("search.field.level"),
          c.level,
          LEVELS.map((l) => ({ value: l, label: t(`search.level.${l}`) })),
          (level) => set({ DashaAt: { ...c, level } }),
        )}
        {grahaSet(t("search.field.lords"), c.lords, (lords) => set({ DashaAt: { ...c, lords } }))}
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.CharaKaraka) {
    const c = cond.CharaKaraka;
    fields = (
      <>
        {select(
          "role",
          t("search.field.role"),
          c.role,
          KARAKA_ROLE_IDS.map((r) => ({ value: r, label: `${KARAKA_ROLES[r].short} · ${KARAKA_ROLES[r].name}` })),
          (role) => set({ CharaKaraka: { ...c, role } }),
        )}
        {grahaSet(t("search.field.grahas"), c.grahas, (grahas) => set({ CharaKaraka: { ...c, grahas } }))}
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.ArudhaInSign) {
    const c = cond.ArudhaInSign;
    fields = (
      <>
        {houseSelect("pada", t("search.field.pada"), c.pada, (pada) => set({ ArudhaInSign: { ...c, pada } }), arudhaLabel)}
        {signSet(c.signs, (signs) => set({ ArudhaInSign: { ...c, signs } }))}
      </>
    );
  } else if (cond.YogaPresent) {
    const c = cond.YogaPresent;
    fields = (
      <CheckSet
        legend={t("search.field.yogas")}
        fold={`${t("search.field.yogas")} · ${count(c.ids.length)}`}
        options={YOGA_IDS.map((y) => ({ value: y, label: t(`yogas.name.${y}`) }))}
        selected={c.ids}
        onToggle={(y) => set({ YogaPresent: { ids: toggle(c.ids, y, YOGA_IDS) } })}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <fieldset className="search-condition" data-condition={index} data-kind={kind}>
      <legend>{t("search.conditionN", { n: index + 1 })}</legend>
      <div className="search-condition-head">
        {select(
          "kind",
          t("search.kindLabel"),
          kind,
          CONDITION_KINDS.map((k) => ({ value: k, label: t(`search.kind.${k}`) })),
          (k: ConditionKind) => onChange({ ...value, cond: defaultCondition(k) }),
        )}
        <label>
          <input type="checkbox" checked={value.negate} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, negate: e.target.checked })} />
          {t("search.negate")}
        </label>
        <button type="button" onClick={onRemove}>
          {t("search.remove")}
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="search-condition-fields">{fields}</div>
      {issue !== null && (
        <p className="chart-toolbar-hint" data-issue={issue}>
          {t(`search.issue.${issue}`)}
        </p>
      )}
    </fieldset>
  );
}
````

**Yeni dosya:** `app/ui/search/ChartSearch.tsx`

````tsx
// Harita araması yüzeyi (ileri modüller spec'i §12 "Arayüz"): sağdan kayan, tam yükseklikte bir
// pencere. Pano paneli değildir: bir haritaya değil danışan veritabanına aittir. Koşul satırları,
// kapsam, kayıtlı aramalar, "Ara" / "Durdur" ve geldikçe listelenen sonuçlar. Sonuç satırı
// haritayı açar; "Sonuçları etiketle" onay penceresinden sonra toplu yazar.
//
// Arayüz hesap yapmaz (C4): koşulları Rust değerlendirir; buradaki denetim yalnızca biçimdir.
// Sorgu ve sonuçlar (adlar, sayılar, harita kimlikleri) loglanmaz.
import { useEffect, useId, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type RefObject } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { api } from "../api";
import type { ChartQuery, CommandError, QueryCondition, QueryScope, SavedSearch } from "../bindings";
import { errorMessage } from "../i18n/errors";
import { useModule } from "../modules/useModule";
import { ConfirmDialog } from "../shell/ConfirmDialog";
import { Dialog } from "../shell/Dialog";
import { ConditionRow } from "./ConditionRow";
import { EXAMPLE_CONDITION, hitSummary, MAX_CONDITIONS, MAX_SAVED_SEARCHES, MAX_SCOPE_TAGS, defaultCondition, parseTags, queryIssue, queryKey, SCOPE_KINDS } from "./model";
import "./search.css";
import { useChartSearch, type ChartSearch as Search } from "./useChartSearch";

/** Listede çizilen en çok eşleşme; fazlası sayılır ve koşulların daraltılması önerilir. */
export const MAX_SHOWN_HITS = 500;

export interface ChartSearchProps {
  /** Koşullar kabukta durur: yüzey kapanıp açılınca form yerindedir. */
  query: ChartQuery;
  onQueryChange(query: ChartQuery): void;
  onClose(): void;
  /** Sonuç satırı: haritayı açar (kabuk yüzeyi kapatır). */
  onOpenChart(clientId: number, chartId: number): void;
  /** Toplu etiketlemeden sonra liste görünümleri yenilenir. */
  onTagged(): void;
}

export function ChartSearch({ query, onQueryChange, onClose, onOpenChart, onTagged }: ChartSearchProps) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const id = useId();
  const search = useChartSearch();
  const running = search.status === "running";
  const addButton = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const runButton = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const [tagsText, setTagsText] = useState(() => query.scope.tags.join(", "));

  const setConditions = (all_of: QueryCondition[]) => onQueryChange({ ...query, all_of });
  const setScope = (patch: Partial<QueryScope>) => onQueryChange({ ...query, scope: { ...query.scope, ...patch } });
  const issue = queryIssue(query);
  const empty = query.all_of.length === 0;
  const why = empty ? "noConditions" : issue !== null ? "incomplete" : null;
  const full = query.all_of.length >= MAX_CONDITIONS;
  // Liste, başlatıldığı sorguya aittir: form sonradan değişirse arama durdurulur ve bu söylenir.
  const key = queryKey(query);
  const stale = search.query !== null && queryKey(search.query) !== key;
  const { stop } = search;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (running && stale) stop();
  }, [running, stale, stop]);

  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (why !== null || running) return;
    search.start(query);
  };
  const remove = (index: number) => {
    // Silinen satırın düğmesi DOM'dan gider: odak "Koşul ekle"ye taşınır.
    addButton.current?.focus();
    setConditions(query.all_of.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  return (
    <Dialog title={t("search.title")} variant="sheet" onClose={onClose}>
      <form className="search-form" aria-label={t("search.form")} onSubmit={submit}>
        <SavedSearches
          query={query}
          saveWhy={why}
          onLoad={(q) => {
            setTagsText(q.scope.tags.join(", "));
            onQueryChange(q);
          }}
        />
        <p className="chart-toolbar-hint">{t("search.allOfHint")}</p>
        {query.all_of.map((c, i) => (
          <ConditionRow key={i} index={i} value={c} onChange={(next) => setConditions(query.all_of.map((x, j) => (j === i ? next : x)))} onRemove={() => remove(i)} />
        ))}
        {empty && <p className="panel-message">{t("search.emptyHint")}</p>}
        <div className="search-toolbar">
          <button
            ref={addButton}
            type="button"
            data-autofocus
            aria-disabled={full ? true : undefined}
            aria-describedby={full ? `${id}-full` : undefined}
            onClick={() => {
              if (!full) setConditions([...query.all_of, empty ? EXAMPLE_CONDITION : { cond: defaultCondition("GrahaInSign"), negate: false }]);
            }}
          >
            {t(empty ? "search.addExample" : "search.add")}
          </button>
          {full && (
            <span id={`${id}-full`} className="chart-toolbar-hint">
              {t("search.full", { max: MAX_CONDITIONS })}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        <fieldset className="search-scope">
          <legend>{t("search.scope.title")}</legend>
          <span className="search-field">
            <label htmlFor={`${id}-tags`}>{t("search.scope.tags")}</label>
            <input
              id={`${id}-tags`}
              type="text"
              value={tagsText}
              aria-describedby={`${id}-tags-hint`}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setTagsText(e.target.value);
                setScope({ tags: parseTags(e.target.value) });
              }}
            />
            <span id={`${id}-tags-hint`} className="chart-toolbar-hint">
              {t("search.scope.tagsHint", { max: MAX_SCOPE_TAGS })}
            </span>
          </span>
          <span className="search-field">
            <label htmlFor={`${id}-kind`}>{t("search.scope.kind")}</label>
            <select id={`${id}-kind`} value={query.scope.kind ?? ""} onChange={(e) => setScope({ kind: SCOPE_KINDS.find((k) => k === e.target.value) ?? null })}>
              <option value="">{t("search.scope.kindAny")}</option>
              {SCOPE_KINDS.map((k) => (
                <option key={k} value={k}>
                  {t(`search.scope.${k}`)}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </span>
          <label>
            <input type="checkbox" checked={query.scope.exclude_prashna} onChange={(e) => setScope({ exclude_prashna: e.target.checked })} />
            {t("search.scope.excludePrashna")}
          </label>
        </fieldset>
        <div className="search-toolbar">
          {/* Kullanılamazken yerel `disabled` değildir: odaklanır ve nedeni görünür bir satırdır. */}
          <button ref={runButton} type="submit" aria-disabled={why !== null || running ? true : undefined} aria-describedby={why !== null ? `${id}-why` : undefined}>
            {t("search.run")}
          </button>
          {running && <StopButton onStop={search.stop} fallback={runButton} />}
          {why !== null && (
            <span id={`${id}-why`} className="chart-toolbar-hint">
              {why === "incomplete" && issue !== null ? t("search.why.incomplete", { n: issue.row + 1 }) : t("search.why.noConditions")}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </form>
      <Results search={search} stale={stale} onRetry={() => {
          // "Yeniden dene" yeni arama başlayınca DOM'dan gider: odak önce "Ara"ya taşınır.
          runButton.current?.focus();
          runButton.current?.click();
        }} onOpenChart={onOpenChart} onTagged={onTagged} />
      <div className="dialog-actions">
        <button type="button" onClick={onClose}>
          {t("search.close")}
        </button>
      </div>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

/**
 * "Durdur": arama bitince ya da durunca DOM'dan gider. Odak ondaysa gövdeye düşmesin diye
 * "Ara" düğmesine taşınır (layout effect'in temizliği düğüm kaldırılmadan önce koşar).
 */
function StopButton({ onStop, fallback }: { onStop(): void; fallback: RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null> }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const ref = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const button = ref.current;
    return () => {
      if (button !== null && document.activeElement === button) fallback.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    };
  }, [fallback]);
  return (
    <button ref={ref} type="button" onClick={onStop}>
      {t("search.stop")}
    </button>
  );
}

/** Kayıtlı aramalar: seç (koşulları forma yükler), adlandırıp sakla, sil. Yalnızca koşullar saklanır. */
function SavedSearches({ query, saveWhy: queryWhy, onLoad }: { query: ChartQuery; saveWhy: string | null; onLoad(query: ChartQuery): void }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const id = useId();
  const loaded = useModule("saved-searches", () => api.listSavedSearches());
  // Kayıttan ve silmeden sonra liste yerinde güncellenir (yeniden okunmaz: seçici DOM'da durur).
  const [written, setWritten] = useState<SavedSearch[] | null>(null);
  const stored = written ?? loaded.data;
  const known = stored !== null;
  const list = stored ?? [];
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState<CommandError | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const select = useRef<HTMLSelectElement>(null);
  const error = failed ?? loaded.error;

  // Store'un karşılaştırmasıyla aynı (`to_lowercase`): arayüz dilinden bağımsız.
  const same = (a: string, b: string) => a.trim().toLowerCase() === b.trim().toLowerCase();
  const trimmed = name.trim();
  const existing = list.find((s) => same(s.name, trimmed));
  const unread = known ? null : loaded.error ? "unread" : "loading";
  const saveWhy = unread ?? (queryWhy !== null ? "queryIncomplete" : trimmed === "" ? "nameNeeded" : !existing && list.length >= MAX_SAVED_SEARCHES ? "capReached" : null);
  const removeWhy = unread ?? (selected === null ? "selectToRemove" : null);

  const save = async () => {
    if (saveWhy !== null || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await api.saveSearch({ name: trimmed, query });
    setBusy(false);
    if (r.status === "error") return setFailed(r.error);
    setFailed(null);
    const saved = r.data;
    setWritten([...list.filter((s) => s.id !== saved.id), saved].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name)));
    setSelected(saved.id);
    setName(saved.name);
  };
  const remove = async () => {
    if (removeWhy !== null || selected === null || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await api.deleteSavedSearch(selected);
    setBusy(false);
    // Başka bir yerden silinmiş kayıt da listeden düşer.
    if (r.status === "error" && r.error.code !== "not_found") return setFailed(r.error);
    setFailed(null);
    setWritten(list.filter((s) => s.id !== selected));
    setSelected(null);
    setName("");
    // "Sil" seçim boşalınca kullanılamaz olur: odak seçiciye taşınır.
    select.current?.focus();
  };

  return (
    <div className="search-toolbar search-saved">
      <span className="search-field">
        <label htmlFor={`${id}-saved`}>{t("search.saved.label")}</label>
        <select
          ref={select}
          id={`${id}-saved`}
          value={selected ?? ""}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const found = list.find((s) => String(s.id) === e.target.value);
            setSelected(found?.id ?? null);
            setName(found?.name ?? "");
            if (found) onLoad(found.query);
          }}
        >
          <option value="">{t("search.saved.none")}</option>
          {list.map((s) => (
            <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
              {s.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </span>
      <span className="search-field">
        <label htmlFor={`${id}-name`}>{t("search.saved.name")}</label>
        <input
          id={`${id}-name`}
          type="text"
          value={name}
          maxLength={200}
          onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            // Enter bu alanda aramayı saklar; çevredeki arama formunu göndermez.
            if (e.key !== "Enter") return;
            e.preventDefault();
            void save();
          }}
        />
      </span>
      <button type="button" aria-disabled={saveWhy !== null || busy ? true : undefined} aria-describedby={saveWhy !== null ? `${id}-save-why` : undefined} onClick={() => void save()}>
        {t("search.saved.save")}
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-disabled={removeWhy !== null || busy ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={removeWhy === null ? undefined : removeWhy === saveWhy ? `${id}-save-why` : `${id}-remove-why`}
        onClick={() => void remove()}
      >
        {t("search.saved.remove")}
      </button>
      {error && (
        <span className="panel-error" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(t, i18n, error)}
        </span>
      )}
      {!known && loaded.error && (
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            // Düğme yeniden okuma başlayınca kalkar: odak seçiciye taşınır.
            select.current?.focus();
            loaded.retry();
          }}
        >
          {t("search.saved.reload")}
        </button>
      )}
      {saveWhy !== null && (
        <span id={`${id}-save-why`} className="chart-toolbar-hint">
          {t(`search.saved.${saveWhy}`, { max: MAX_SAVED_SEARCHES })}
        </span>
      )}
      {removeWhy !== null && removeWhy !== saveWhy && (
        <span id={`${id}-remove-why`} className="chart-toolbar-hint">
          {t(`search.saved.${removeWhy}`)}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Results({ search, stale, onRetry, onOpenChart, onTagged }: { search: Search; stale: boolean; onRetry(): void; onOpenChart(clientId: number, chartId: number): void; onTagged(): void }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const listId = useId();
  const { status, hits, scanned, total } = search;
  const sent = search.query;
  if (status === "idle" || sent === null) return <p className="panel-message">{t("search.idle")}</p>;
  const shown = hits.slice(0, MAX_SHOWN_HITS);
  const counts = { n: hits.length, scanned, total: total ?? 0 };
  // İlk sayfadan önce toplam bilinmez: sayı uydurulmaz, yalnızca "Aranıyor…" yazılır.
  const summary = total === null ? (status === "running" ? t("search.summary.starting") : status === "stopped" ? t("search.summary.stoppedEarly") : null) : t(`search.summary.${status}`, counts);
  const finished = status === "done" && !stale;
  return (
    <section className="search-results" aria-labelledby={listId}>
      <h3 id={listId}>{t("search.results")}</h3>
      {/* İlerleme ve sonuç sayısı tek bir canlı bölgede: her sayfada yeniden okunur. */}
      <p className="panel-message" role="status" data-summary>
        {summary ?? ""}
      </p>
      {status === "running" && total !== null && <progress className="search-progress" max={Math.max(total, 1)} value={scanned} aria-label={t("search.progress")} />}
      {search.skippedTimeUnknown > 0 && <p className="panel-notice">{t("search.skippedTimeUnknown", { n: search.skippedTimeUnknown })}</p>}
      {search.failed > 0 && <p className="panel-notice">{t("search.failed", { n: search.failed })}</p>}
      {stale && (
        <p className="panel-notice" role="note">
          {t("search.stale")}
        </p>
      )}
      {search.error && (
        <p className="panel-error" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(t, i18n, search.error)}{" "}
          <button type="button" onClick={onRetry}>
            {t("search.retry")}
          </button>
        </p>
      )}
      {status === "done" && hits.length === 0 && <p className="panel-message">{t(scanned === 0 ? "search.noCharts" : "search.noHits", { scanned })}</p>}
      {shown.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="search-hits">
          {shown.map((h) => {
            const chart = h.chart_label === "" ? t("search.unlabeled") : h.chart_label;
            return (
              <li key={h.chart_id}>
                <button type="button" className="search-hit" aria-label={t("search.open", { client: h.client_name, chart })} onClick={() => onOpenChart(h.client_id, h.chart_id)}>
                  <span className="search-hit-name">{h.client_name}</span>
                  <span className="search-hit-chart">{chart}</span>
                  <span className="search-hit-facts">{hitSummary(t, sent, h.matched)}</span>
                </button>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
      {hits.length > shown.length && <p className="panel-notice">{t("search.capped", { shown: shown.length, n: hits.length })}</p>}
      {hits.length > 0 && <TagResults chartIds={hits.map((h) => h.chart_id)} finished={finished} onTagged={onTagged} />}
    </section>
  );
}

/** "Sonuçları etiketle": etiket adı, sonra etiketi ve etkilenecek harita sayısını gösteren onay. */
function TagResults({ chartIds, finished, onTagged }: { chartIds: number[]; finished: boolean; onTagged(): void }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const id = useId();
  const [tag, setTag] = useState("");
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<CommandError | null>(null);
  const trimmed = tag.trim();
  const why = !finished ? "notFinished" : trimmed === "" ? "tagNeeded" : trimmed.includes(",") ? "oneTag" : null;
  const apply = async () => {
    setConfirming(false);
    setBusy(true);
    const r = await api.tagCharts({ chart_ids: chartIds, tag: trimmed });
    setBusy(false);
    if (r.status === "error") {
      setDone(null);
      return setError(r.error);
    }
    setError(null);
    setDone(r.data);
    onTagged();
  };
  return (
    <div className="search-toolbar search-tag">
      <span className="search-field">
        <label htmlFor={`${id}-tag`}>{t("search.tag.label")}</label>
        <input
          id={`${id}-tag`}
          type="text"
          value={tag}
          maxLength={64}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setTag(e.target.value);
            setDone(null);
          }}
        />
      </span>
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-disabled={why !== null || busy ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={why !== null ? `${id}-why` : undefined}
        onClick={() => {
          if (why === null && !busy) setConfirming(true);
        }}
      >
        {t("search.tag.apply")}
      </button>
      {why !== null && (
        <span id={`${id}-why`} className="chart-toolbar-hint">
          {t(`search.tag.${why}`)}
        </span>
      )}
      <span role="status">{done === null ? "" : t("search.tag.done", { n: done, tag: trimmed })}</span>
      {error && (
        <span className="panel-error" role="alert">
          {errorMessage(t, i18n, error)}
        </span>
      )}
      {confirming && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={t("search.tag.confirmTitle")}
          message={t("search.tag.confirm", { tag: trimmed, n: chartIds.length })}
          confirmLabel={t("search.tag.confirmAction", { n: chartIds.length })}
          onConfirm={() => void apply()}
          onCancel={() => setConfirming(false)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
````

**Yeni dosya:** `app/ui/search/search.css`

````css
/* Harita araması yüzeyi (ileri modüller spec'i §12). Renkler yalnızca token'lardan gelir (C9). */
.search-form,
.search-results {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
}
.search-toolbar {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px 12px;
  color: var(--text-2);
}
.search-field,
.search-form label {
  display: inline-flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
}
.search-condition,
.search-scope {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 6px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
}
.search-condition-head,
.search-condition-fields {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 6px 12px;
}
.search-checks {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 2px 12px;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 4px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
}
.search-fold {
  flex: 1 1 100%;
}
.search-fold summary {
  cursor: default;
  color: var(--text-2);
}
.search-form button[aria-disabled="true"],
.search-tag button[aria-disabled="true"] {
  color: var(--text-2);
}
.search-progress {
  width: 100%;
}
.search-hits {
  list-style: none;
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
}
.search-hit {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(8rem, 1fr) minmax(6rem, 1fr) 2fr;
  gap: 8px;
  width: 100%;
  padding: 4px 6px;
  border: 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
  font: inherit;
  text-align: start;
}
.search-hit:hover,
.search-hit:focus-visible {
  background: var(--select);
}
.search-hit-name {
  font-weight: var(--fw-medium);
}
.search-hit-facts {
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}
````

### Task 6: Arayüz: olay araması paneli

Spec §12 "Arayüz" (olay araması); UI spec §3.3, §5, §8; K12; D23, D24, D27. Panel Plan 20'nin yer seçicisini (`PlaceControls`, `usePanelPlace`) ve Task 5'in `CheckSet`'ini kullanır.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/ui/modules/useEventSearch.ts`, `app/ui/modules/useEventSearch.test.tsx`, `app/ui/panels/EventSearchPanel.tsx`, `app/ui/panels/EventSearchPanel.test.tsx`
- Modify: `app/ui/layout/model.ts`, `app/ui/layout/model.test.ts`, `app/ui/panels/registry.ts`, `app/ui/panels/panels.test.tsx`, `app/ui/i18n/tr.ts`, `app/ui/i18n/en.ts`, `app/ui/i18n/fr.ts`
- Test: iki yeni dosya (4 + 10); `layout/model.test.ts` ve `panels/panels.test.tsx` (var olan testlere beklenti)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5'in `api.eventSearch`, `api.eventSearchCancel`, `search/ids.nextSearchId`, `search/CheckSet`, `search/model` (`EVENT_KINDS`, `MAX_EVENTS`, `toggle`), `search/search.css`; `modules/input.chartInput`, `modules/muhurta.muhurtaPlace`, `panels/MuhurtaPlace` (`PlaceControls`, `PlaceMissing`, `usePanelPlace`), `panels/states` (`PanelError`, `usePanelLoadPhase`), `panels/format` (`formatJdDate`, `formatJdClock`, `formatJdMinute`), `transit/store.useTransitControl`, `session/birth.timeUnknown`, `session/store.useSession`.
- Produces:
  - `modules/useEventSearch.ts`: `useEventSearch(scope: string): EventSearch` (`status: "idle" | "running" | "done" | "stopped" | "error"`, `view: EventSearchView | null`, `error`, `start(spec)`, `stop()`).
  - `panels/EventSearchPanel.tsx`: `EventSearchPanel({ panel })`, `parseSet<T>(raw, allowed, fallback): T[]`, `DEFAULT_EVENT_KINDS`, `DEFAULT_EVENT_GRAHAS`, `MAX_SHOWN_EVENTS` (500).
  - `layout/model.ts`: panel türü `"event_search"`; `MODULE_PANELS` girdisi `{ kind: "event_search", w: 7, h: 18 }`.
  - i18n: `panel.event_search`, `eventSearch.*`.

**İnceleme sonrası kararlar (bu görevin kodu bunlarla yazılıdır):**
- **Kesilme notu anı söyler.** `scanned_to_jd_ut` son olayın anıdır; not onu dakikasıyla ve o olayın kendi `offset_secs` değeriyle yazar ("2026-12-09 06:21 anından sonrası taranmadı"). Aralık sonunun ofseti kullanılmaz: yaz saati geçişi ve gece yarısı yakınında tarih bir gün kayardı; yalnızca tarih yazmak o günün sonraki olaylarının tarandığını ima ederdi.
- **Tek durum bölgesi.** Sonuç alanının başında hep DOM'da duran bir `role="status"` kabı vardır: başta boştur, arama sürerken "Aranıyor…", durdurulunca "Arama durduruldu.", bitince olay sayısı satırı (`data-summary`; olay yoksa "Bu aralıkta olay yok…") ile kesilme ve "ilk 500" notlarını taşır. Araç çubuğundaki ayrı durum yazısı yoktur. Boş durum yönergesi ve bayat liste notu (`role="note"`) bölgenin dışındadır.
- **"Ara" sürerken.** Düğme `aria-disabled`'dır ve `aria-describedby` ile durum bölgesine bağlanır: neden ("Aranıyor…") görünür bir satırdır ve gecikmesiz yazılır.
- **Bayatlık anahtarı isteğin tamamıdır (D24).** Anahtar gönderilecek `EventSearchSpec`'in JSON'udur: yer, tarihler, türler ve grahaların yanında haritanın hesap ayarları ve (gönderiliyorsa) doğum verisi. Yalnızca bellekte karşılaştırılır.
- **Yeni arama önceki listeyi kaldırır.** Liste yalnızca kendi isteğiyle durur; arama başlayınca tablo DOM'dan çıkar (odak o an formdadır). Kancanın başlık yorumu bunu söyler.
- **Çoğul.** en ve fr özet satırı sayıyı addan sonra yazar ("Events: {{n}} · …", "Événements : {{n}} · …"); tr "{{n}} olay" olarak kalır.
- **Hata alanı.** `invalid_search_input`'un alan adı panelde `eventSearch.field.*` ile arayüz dilinde yazılır (yedi alan: `kinds`, `grahas`, `natal`, `range`, `place`, `from_date`, `to_date`); `range` kuralı da söyler.

**Yeni dosya:** `app/ui/modules/useEventSearch.test.tsx`

````tsx
import { act, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { beforeEach, expect, test, vi } from "vitest";

vi.mock("../api", () => ({ api: { eventSearch: vi.fn(), eventSearchCancel: vi.fn() } }));

import { api, type Result } from "../api";
import type { EventSearchView } from "../bindings";
import { fixture } from "../test/fixture";
import { useEventSearch, type EventSearch } from "./useEventSearch";

const FX = fixture().search;
const SPEC = FX.event_spec;

function deferred() {
  let resolve!: (r: Result<EventSearchView>) => void;
  const promise = new Promise<Result<EventSearchView>>((r) => (resolve = r));
  return { promise, resolve };
}
const ok = (view: EventSearchView): Result<EventSearchView> => ({ status: "ok", data: view });

let search: EventSearch;
function Probe({ scope = "1" }: { scope?: string }) {
  search = useEventSearch(scope);
  return <span data-testid="state">{`${search.status} ${search.view?.events.length ?? "-"} ${search.error?.code ?? "-"}`}</span>;
}
const state = () => screen.getByTestId("state").textContent;
const calls = () => vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mock.calls;

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockReset();
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearchCancel).mockReset();
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearchCancel).mockResolvedValue({ status: "ok", data: null });
});

test("a search runs once with a new id and shows what came back", async () => {
  const reply = deferred();
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockReturnValueOnce(reply.promise);
  render(<Probe />);
  expect(state()).toBe("idle - -");
  act(() => search.start(SPEC));
  expect(state()).toBe("running - -");
  expect(calls()).toHaveLength(1);
  expect(calls()[0][0]).toBe(SPEC);
  await act(async () => reply.resolve(ok(FX.events)));
  expect(state()).toBe(`done ${FX.events.events.length} -`);
  expect(api.eventSearchCancel).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
});

test("a new search cancels the old one and drops its late answer", async () => {
  const [old, fresh] = [deferred(), deferred()];
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockReturnValueOnce(old.promise).mockReturnValueOnce(fresh.promise);
  render(<Probe />);
  act(() => search.start(SPEC));
  act(() => search.start({ ...SPEC, grahas: ["Saturn"] }));
  expect(calls()[1][1]).not.toBe(calls()[0][1]);
  expect(api.eventSearchCancel).toHaveBeenCalledWith(calls()[0][1]);
  await act(async () => old.resolve(ok(FX.events)));
  expect(state()).toBe("running - -");
  await act(async () => fresh.resolve(ok({ ...FX.events, events: [] })));
  expect(state()).toBe("done 0 -");
});

test("stopping tells Rust and the stale answer of a stopped search is not an error", async () => {
  const reply = deferred();
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockReturnValueOnce(reply.promise);
  render(<Probe />);
  act(() => search.start(SPEC));
  act(() => search.stop());
  expect(state()).toBe("stopped - -");
  expect(api.eventSearchCancel).toHaveBeenCalledWith(calls()[0][1]);
  await act(async () => reply.resolve({ status: "error", error: { code: "search_stale", params: {} } }));
  expect(state()).toBe("stopped - -");
  // Başka bir yüzeyin aramasıyla geçersiz olan arama da durmuş sayılır.
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockResolvedValueOnce({ status: "error", error: { code: "search_stale", params: {} } });
  await act(async () => search.start(SPEC));
  expect(state()).toBe("stopped - -");
});

test("an error shows no earlier events, and another chart cancels and clears", async () => {
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockResolvedValueOnce(ok(FX.events));
  const view = render(<Probe />);
  await act(async () => search.start(SPEC));
  expect(state()).toBe(`done ${FX.events.events.length} -`);
  // Hata: önceki aramanın olayları gösterilmez.
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockResolvedValueOnce({ status: "error", error: { code: "invalid_search_input", params: { field: "range" } } });
  await act(async () => search.start(SPEC));
  expect(state()).toBe("error - invalid_search_input");
  // Hatadan sonraki arama boş başlar.
  const pending = deferred();
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockReturnValueOnce(pending.promise);
  act(() => search.start(SPEC));
  expect(state()).toBe("running - -");
  const id = calls()[2][1];
  view.rerender(<Probe scope="2" />);
  expect(state()).toBe("idle - -");
  expect(api.eventSearchCancel).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(id);
  await act(async () => pending.resolve(ok(FX.events)));
  expect(state()).toBe("idle - -");
});
````

**Yeni dosya:** `app/ui/panels/EventSearchPanel.test.tsx`

````tsx
import { act, fireEvent, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { beforeEach, expect, test, vi } from "vitest";

vi.mock("../api", () => ({ api: { eventSearch: vi.fn(), eventSearchCancel: vi.fn(), searchPlaces: vi.fn(), setAppPrefs: vi.fn() } }));

import { api, type Result } from "../api";
import type { AppPrefs, EventSearchView, OpenedClient } from "../bindings";
import { en } from "../i18n/en";
import { fr } from "../i18n/fr";
import { tr } from "../i18n/tr";
import type { Panel } from "../layout/model";
import { chartInput } from "../modules/input";
import { EVENT_KINDS } from "../search/model";
import { fixture, unknownTimeOpened } from "../test/fixture";
import { renderWithStores } from "../test/render";
import { useSession, type Session } from "../session/store";
import { useTransitControl, type Transit } from "../transit/store";
import { useLayoutStore } from "../view/store";
import { DEFAULT_EVENT_GRAHAS, DEFAULT_EVENT_KINDS, EventSearchPanel, MAX_SHOWN_EVENTS, parseSet } from "./EventSearchPanel";

const FX = fixture();
const VIEW = FX.search.events;
const ok = (view: EventSearchView): Result<EventSearchView> => ({ status: "ok", data: view });
const LAYOUT = (settings: Record<string, unknown>) => {
  const panel: Panel = { id: "es", kind: "event_search", x: 0, y: 0, w: 7, h: 18, settings };
  return { schema_version: 1, active_layout_id: "user:t", layouts: [{ id: "user:t", name: "T", builtin: false, panels: [panel] }] };
};

let transit: Transit;
let session: Session;
/** Paneli düzen deposundan okur: ayar değişince panel yeni ayarla yeniden çizilir (pano gibi). */
function LivePanel() {
  const { active } = useLayoutStore();
  transit = useTransitControl();
  session = useSession();
  return <EventSearchPanel panel={active.panels[0]} />;
}
function renderPanel(settings: Record<string, unknown> = {}, { opened = fixture().opened, prefs = FX.prefs.prefs }: { opened?: OpenedClient | null; prefs?: AppPrefs | null } = {}) {
  return renderWithStores(<LivePanel />, { layout: LAYOUT(settings), opened, prefs });
}
function deferred() {
  let resolve!: (r: Result<EventSearchView>) => void;
  const promise = new Promise<Result<EventSearchView>>((r) => (resolve = r));
  return { promise, resolve };
}
const run = () => screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ara" });
const box = (name: string) => screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name });
const summary = () => document.querySelector("[data-summary]")?.textContent;
const rows = () => within(screen.getByRole("table", { name: "Olaylar" })).getAllByRole("row").slice(1);
const cells = (row: HTMLElement) => [...row.querySelectorAll("th, td")].map((c) => c.textContent);
const settle = () => act(async () => {});
const sent = () => vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mock.calls.at(-1)![0];

beforeEach(() => {
  for (const f of Object.values(api)) vi.mocked(f).mockReset();
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearchCancel).mockResolvedValue({ status: "ok", data: null });
  vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockResolvedValue(ok(VIEW));
});

test("a new panel starts with the slow grahas, the chart's place and nothing searched", () => {
  renderPanel();
  const form = screen.getByRole("form", { name: "Olay araması ayarları" });
  expect(within(form).getByRole("combobox", { name: "Yer" })).toHaveDisplayValue("Haritanın doğum yeri");
  for (const name of ["Başlangıç tarihi", "Bitiş tarihi"]) expect(within(form).getByLabelText(name)).toHaveValue("");
  const kinds = within(form).getByRole("group", { name: "Olay türleri" });
  expect(within(kinds).getAllByRole("checkbox")).toHaveLength(EVENT_KINDS.length);
  expect(within(kinds).getAllByRole("checkbox", { checked: true }).map((c) => c.parentElement?.textContent)).toEqual(["Burç girişi", "R başlangıcı", "R bitişi"]);
  const grahas = within(form).getByRole("group", { name: "Grahalar" });
  expect(within(grahas).getAllByRole("checkbox", { checked: true }).map((c) => c.parentElement?.textContent)).toEqual(["Jüpiter", "Satürn", "Rahu"]);
  expect(screen.getByText(/Türleri, grahaları ve aralığı seçip/)).toBeInTheDocument();

  expect(screen.queryByRole("table")).toBeNull();
  // Durum bölgesi baştan DOM'dadır ve boştur: sonradan yazılan metin duyurulur.

});

test("the request carries the settings, and the natal chart only when a natal kind is selected", async () => {
  const opened = fixture().opened;
  const { persistence } = renderPanel();
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Başlangıç tarihi"), { target: { value: "2026-11-26" } });
  fireEvent.click(run());
  await settle();
  const birth = opened.chart_record.birth;
  expect(sent()).toEqual({
    from_date: "2026-11-26",
    to_date: null,
    place: { lat: birth.place.lat, lon: birth.place.lon, tz: birth.tz },
    kinds: [...DEFAULT_EVENT_KINDS],
    grahas: [...DEFAULT_EVENT_GRAHAS],
    settings: opened.chart.meta.settings,
    natal: null,
  });
  // Seçim panelin ayarıdır: Rust'ın sırasıyla saklanır.
  fireEvent.click(box("Doğum boylamına geliş"));
  fireEvent.click(box("Güneş"));
  await settle();
  const saved = persistence.saved.at(-1)?.viewState.panel_layout!.layouts.find((l) => l.id === "user:t")!.panels[0].settings;
  expect(saved).toMatchObject({ kinds: ["SignIngress", "StationRetrograde", "StationDirect", "TransitToNatal"], grahas: ["Sun", "Jupiter", "Saturn", "Rahu"] });
  fireEvent.click(run());
  await settle();
  expect(sent().natal).toEqual(chartInput(opened));
  expect(sent().grahas).toEqual(["Sun", "Jupiter", "Saturn", "Rahu"]);
});

  expect(rows()).toHaveLength(VIEW.events.length);

  expect(cells(rows()[1]).slice(1)).toEqual(["Satürn: R bitiyor", "Bal"]);
  expect(cells(rows()[2]).slice(1)).toEqual(["Jüpiter: R başlıyor", "Asl"]);

  expect(screen.getByText(/Yöntem: event_search\.v1/)).toBeInTheDocument();
});

test("while searching the panel says so and stopping keeps the focus on the search button", async () => {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
  try {
    const pending = deferred();
    vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mockReset().mockReturnValueOnce(pending.promise);
    renderPanel();
    fireEvent.click(run());
    expect(run()).toHaveAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
    // Kullanılamamanın nedeni görünür ve düğmeye bağlıdır; aynı bölge sonra sonucu taşır.
    const status = screen.getByRole("status");
    expect(status).toHaveTextContent("Aranıyor…");
    expect(run()).toHaveAccessibleDescription("Aranıyor…");
    await act(async () => vi.advanceTimersByTime(150));
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toBe(status);
    const stop = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Durdur" });
    stop.focus();
    fireEvent.click(stop);
    expect(api.eventSearchCancel).toHaveBeenCalledWith(vi.mocked(api.eventSearch).mock.calls[0][1]);
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Durdur" })).toBeNull();
    expect(run()).toHaveFocus();
    expect(screen.getByRole("status")).toBe(status);
    expect(status).toHaveTextContent("Arama durduruldu.");
    expect(run()).not.toHaveAccessibleDescription();
    // Durdurulan aramanın geç gelen yanıtı gösterilmez.
    await act(async () => pending.resolve(ok(VIEW)));
    expect(screen.queryByRole("table")).toBeNull();
  } finally {
    vi.useRealTimers();
  }
});

  expect(status).toHaveTextContent(capped);
});

  expect(parseSet("bozuk", EVENT_KINDS, DEFAULT_EVENT_KINDS)).toEqual([...DEFAULT_EVENT_KINDS]);
````
